+class_name Boss
+extends Node2D
+## «Объект-7»: отдельный босс, без физических тел и нод на атаки.
+
+const RADIUS := 18.0
+const MAX_HP := 24000.0
+const CHARGE_SPEED := 400.0
+const CHARGE_COOLDOWN := 6.0
+const SLAM_COOLDOWN := 9.0
+const ROAR_COOLDOWN := 15.0
+const SLAM_WINDUP := 0.5
+const SLAM_TIME := 0.6
+const SLAM_RADIUS := 150.0
+const SLAM_WIDTH := 20.0
+const ROAR_TIME := 0.8
+const SHED_TIME := 1.2
+const CELL := Vector2(144.0, 144.0)
+
+signal phase_changed(phase: int)
+signal died(pos: Vector2)
+signal charge_telegraph(from: Vector2, to: Vector2, time: float)
+signal slam_started(pos: Vector2)
+signal roar(count: int)
+signal crushed(pos: Vector2, dir: Vector2)
+signal armor_plate(pos: Vector2, dir: Vector2)
+
+var horde: Horde
+var player: Player
+var effects: Effects
+var floor_decals: FloorDecals
+var camera: GameCamera
+var arena: Rect2
+var minutes := 0.0
+
+var hp := MAX_HP
+var phase := 1
+
+enum State { WALK, TELEGRAPH, CHARGE, RECOVER, SLAM, ROAR, SHED }
+
+var _alive := false
+var _state := State.WALK
+var _last_attack := -1
+var _age := 0.0
+var _time := 0.0
+var _walk := 0.0
+var _facing := Vector2.RIGHT
+var _charge_cd := 0.0
+var _slam_cd := 0.0
+var _roar_cd := 0.0
+var _contact_cd := 0.0
+var _flash := 0.0
+var _plate_damage := 0.0
+var _blood_time := 0.0
+var _charges_left := 0
+var _charge_target := Vector2.ZERO
+var _charge_from := Vector2.ZERO
+var _charge_hit := false
+var _telegraph_time := 1.0
+var _recover_time := 0.6
+var _slam_pos := Vector2.ZERO
+var _slam_hit := false
+var _slam_wave := false
+var _wave_previous := 0.0
+var _sheet: Texture2D
+var _flash_material: ShaderMaterial
+var _body: Node2D
+
+
+func _ready() -> void:
+	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://art/boss.png"):
+		_sheet = PixelArt.tex("boss")
+	# Один слой тела отделяет белую вспышку от телеграфов и тени.
+	var shader := Shader.new()
+	shader.code = "shader_type canvas_item; uniform float hit = 0.0; void fragment() { vec4 c = texture(TEXTURE, UV) * COLOR; COLOR = vec4(mix(c.rgb, vec3(1.0), hit), c.a); }"
+	_flash_material = ShaderMaterial.new()
+	_flash_material.shader = shader
+	_body = Node2D.new()
+	_body.material = _flash_material
+	_body.draw.connect(_draw_sprite)
+	add_child(_body)
+	visible = _alive
+
+
+func start(p: Vector2) -> void:
+	hp = MAX_HP
+	phase = 1
+	_alive = true
+	_state = State.WALK
+	_last_attack = -1
+	_age = 0.0
+	_time = 0.0
+	_walk = 0.0
+	_facing = Vector2.RIGHT
+	_charge_cd = 0.0
+	_slam_cd = 0.0
+	_roar_cd = 0.0
+	_contact_cd = 0.0
+	_flash = 0.0
+	_plate_damage = 0.0
+	_blood_time = 0.0
+	_charges_left = 0
+	_charge_hit = false
+	_slam_hit = false
+	_slam_wave = false
+	global_position = _bounded(p)
+	visible = true
+	queue_redraw()
+
+
+func take_damage(amount: float, dir: Vector2, at: Vector2) -> bool:
+	if not _alive or _state == State.SHED or amount <= 0.0:
+		return false
+	var damage := amount * (0.75 if phase == 1 else 1.0)
+	hp = maxf(hp - damage, 0.0)
+	_flash = 0.08
+	if hp <= 0.0:
+		_alive = false
+		visible = false
+		_charges_left = 0
+		_slam_wave = false
+		died.emit(global_position)
+		return true
+	if phase == 1:
+		_plate_damage += damage
+		while _plate_damage >= 1500.0:
+			_plate_damage -= 1500.0
+			armor_plate.emit(at, dir.normalized() if dir != Vector2.ZERO else _facing)
+		if hp <= MAX_HP * 0.5:
+			_shed_armor()
+	elif effects:
+		effects.blood_hit(at, dir, false)
+	queue_redraw()
+	return false
+
+
+func hit_test(p: Vector2, r: float) -> bool:
+	return _alive and p.distance_squared_to(global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + maxf(r, 0.0), 2.0)
+
+
+func is_alive() -> bool:
+	return _alive
+
+
+func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
+	if not _alive:
+		return
+	global_position = _bounded(global_position)
+	_time += delta
+	_age += delta
+	_flash = maxf(_flash - delta, 0.0)
+	_charge_cd = maxf(_charge_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	_slam_cd = maxf(_slam_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	_roar_cd = maxf(_roar_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	_contact_cd = maxf(_contact_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	if _state == State.SHED:
+		if _age + 0.000001 >= SHED_TIME:
+			_enter(State.WALK)
+	elif player and not player.is_dead:
+		match _state:
+			State.WALK:
+				_choose_attack()
+				if _state == State.WALK:
+					_move_to(global_position.move_toward(player.global_position, (40.0 if phase == 1 else 60.0) * delta))
+					_contact()
+			State.TELEGRAPH:
+				if _age + 0.000001 >= _telegraph_time:
+					_charge_hit = false
+					_enter(State.CHARGE)
+			State.CHARGE:
+				_charge(delta)
+			State.RECOVER:
+				if _age + 0.000001 >= _recover_time:
+					if _charges_left > 0:
+						_begin_telegraph()
+					else:
+						_enter(State.WALK)
+			State.SLAM:
+				_slam()
+			State.ROAR:
+				if _age + 0.000001 >= ROAR_TIME:
+					_roar_cd = ROAR_COOLDOWN
+					_enter(State.WALK)
+					roar.emit(15 if phase == 1 else 25)
+	var moving := _state == State.WALK or _state == State.CHARGE
+	if phase == 2 and moving and effects:
+		_blood_time += delta
+		if _blood_time >= 0.1:
+			_blood_time = 0.0
+			effects.emit(global_position + Vector2.from_angle(randf() * TAU) * 10.0, -_facing * 12.0, 0.3, Effects.BLOOD, 2.5, 5.0, true)
+	queue_redraw()
+
+
+func _choose_attack() -> void:
+	var distance := global_position.distance_to(player.global_position)
+	if distance > 180.0 and _charge_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.CHARGE:
+		_begin_charge_series()
+	elif distance < 120.0 and _slam_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.SLAM:
+		_last_attack = State.SLAM
+		_slam_pos = global_position
+		_slam_hit = false
+		_slam_wave = false
+		_enter(State.SLAM)
+	elif _roar_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.ROAR and _few_runners():
+		_last_attack = State.ROAR
+		_enter(State.ROAR)
+		if effects:
+			effects.blood_burst(global_position + _facing * 18.0, _facing, 18, 80.0, 0.8)
+	elif _charge_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.CHARGE and distance > RADIUS + Player.RADIUS:
+		# Дистанция задаёт приоритет, но не блокирует чередование при живых бегунах.
+		_begin_charge_series()
+
+
+func _begin_charge_series() -> void:
+	_last_attack = State.CHARGE
+	_charges_left = 1 if phase == 1 else 3
+	_begin_telegraph()
+
+
+func _few_runners() -> bool:
+	if horde == null:
+		return true
+	var runners := 0
+	for j in horde.query_radius(global_position, 260.0):
+		if j >= 0 and j < horde.count and horde.dead[j] == 0 and horde.kind[j] == Horde.Kind.RUNNER:
+			runners += 1
+			if runners >= 8:
+				return false
+	return true
+
+
+func _begin_telegraph() -> void:
+	var offset := player.global_position - global_position
+	if offset.length_squared() > 0.000001:
+		_facing = offset.normalized()
+	_charge_from = global_position
+	# Луч до внутренней границы: вся туша остаётся в арене.
+	var inner := _inner_arena()
+	var distance := 100000.0
+	if absf(_facing.x) > 0.000001:
+		var edge := inner.end.x if _facing.x > 0.0 else inner.position.x
+		distance = minf(distance, maxf((edge - global_position.x) / _facing.x, 0.0))
+	if absf(_facing.y) > 0.000001:
+		var edge := inner.end.y if _facing.y > 0.0 else inner.position.y
+		distance = minf(distance, maxf((edge - global_position.y) / _facing.y, 0.0))
+	_charge_target = _bounded(global_position + _facing * distance)
+	_telegraph_time = 1.0 if phase == 1 else 0.7
+	_enter(State.TELEGRAPH)
+	charge_telegraph.emit(_charge_from, _charge_target, _telegraph_time)
+
+
+func _charge(delta: float) -> void:
+	var from := global_position
+	_move_to(from.move_toward(_charge_target, CHARGE_SPEED * delta))
+	var to := global_position
+	if not _charge_hit:
+		var nearest := Geometry2D.get_closest_point_to_segment(player.global_position, from, to)
+		if nearest.distance_squared_to(player.global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + Player.RADIUS, 2.0):
+			_charge_hit = true # один запрос урона на проход, даже при неуязвимости
+			player.take_damage(35.0)
+	if not _alive or _state != State.CHARGE:
+		return
+	if horde:
+		var middle := (from + to) * 0.5
+		for j in horde.query_radius(middle, from.distance_to(to) * 0.5 + RADIUS):
+			if j < 0 or j >= horde.count or horde.dead[j] != 0:
+				continue
+			var p := horde.pos[j]
+			var nearest := Geometry2D.get_closest_point_to_segment(p, from, to)
+			if nearest.distance_squared_to(p) <= pow(RADIUS + horde.radius[j], 2.0):
+				if horde.damage(j, 9999.0, _facing, 300.0, WeaponDefs.Ammo.STANDARD, false, horde.pos[j], true):
+					crushed.emit(p, _facing)
+				if not _alive or _state != State.CHARGE:
+					return
+	if to.distance_squared_to(_charge_target) < 0.000001 or to == from:
+		_charges_left -= 1
+		_recover_time = 0.4 if _charges_left > 0 else 0.6
+		if _charges_left == 0:
+			_charge_cd = CHARGE_COOLDOWN
+		_enter(State.RECOVER)
+
+
+func _slam() -> void:
+	if _age + 0.000001 < SLAM_WINDUP:
+		return
+	if not _slam_wave:
+		_slam_wave = true
+		_slam_cd = SLAM_COOLDOWN * (0.7 if phase == 2 else 1.0)
+		if camera:
+			camera.shake(6.0)
+		slam_started.emit(_slam_pos)
+		if not _alive or _state != State.SLAM:
+			return
+	var radius := SLAM_RADIUS * clampf((_age - SLAM_WINDUP) / SLAM_TIME, 0.0, 1.0)
+	# Относительное движение игрока и фронта волны между двумя тиками.
+	var relative := player.global_position.distance_to(_slam_pos) - radius
+	if not _slam_hit:
+		if absf(relative) <= SLAM_WIDTH * 0.5 + Player.RADIUS or _wave_previous * relative < 0.0:
+			_slam_hit = true
+			player.take_damage(25.0)
+	_wave_previous = relative
+	if _age + 0.000001 >= SLAM_WINDUP + SLAM_TIME:
+		_slam_wave = false
+		_enter(State.WALK)
+
+
+func _contact() -> void:
+	if _contact_cd <= 0.0 and global_position.distance_squared_to(player.global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + Player.RADIUS, 2.0):
+		_contact_cd = 0.8
+		player.take_damage(20.0 if phase == 1 else 25.0)
+
+
+func _shed_armor() -> void:
+	phase = 2
+	_charges_left = 0
+	_slam_wave = false
+	_enter(State.SHED)
+	if camera:
+		camera.shake(9.0)
+	if effects:
+		effects.blood_burst(global_position, _facing, 100, 190.0, PI)
+	for k in 8:
+		var dir := Vector2.from_angle(float(k) * TAU / 8.0)
+		armor_plate.emit(global_position + dir * 12.0, dir)
+	phase_changed.emit(2)
+
+
+func _enter(state: State) -> void:
+	_state = state
+	_age = 0.0
+	if state == State.SLAM:
+		_wave_previous = player.global_position.distance_to(global_position)
+
+
+func _move_to(p: Vector2) -> void:
+	var before := global_position
+	global_position = _bounded(p)
+	var step := global_position - before
+	_walk += step.length()
+	if step.length_squared() > 0.000001:
+		_facing = step.normalized()
+
+
+func _inner_arena() -> Rect2:
+	var margin := Vector2(minf(RADIUS, maxf(arena.size.x, 0.0) * 0.5), minf(RADIUS, maxf(arena.size.y, 0.0) * 0.5))
+	return Rect2(arena.position + margin, (arena.size - margin * 2.0).max(Vector2.ZERO))
+
+
+func _bounded(p: Vector2) -> Vector2:
+	var inner := _inner_arena()
+	return p.clamp(inner.position, inner.end)
+
+
+func _draw() -> void:
+	if not _alive:
+		return
+	_draw_attacks()
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(self, Vector2(1.0, 4.0), 20.0, Color(0.015, 0.012, 0.012, 0.55))
+	if _body:
+		_body.queue_redraw()
+
+
+func _draw_sprite() -> void:
+	var breath := 1.0 + 0.035 * sin(_time * (9.0 if _state == State.RECOVER else 3.0))
+	var scale_factor := Vector2(breath, 1.0 / breath)
+	if _state == State.SLAM and not _slam_wave:
+		scale_factor *= Vector2(1.12, 0.8)
+	if _state == State.SHED:
+		scale_factor *= 1.0 + 0.07 * sin(_age * 45.0)
+	if _sheet:
+		var col := 4 if _state == State.CHARGE else int(_walk / 7.0) % 4
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, _facing.angle(), scale_factor * PixelArt.ART_SCALE)
+		_body.draw_texture_rect_region(_sheet, Rect2(-CELL * 0.5, CELL), Rect2(Vector2(col, phase - 1) * CELL, CELL))
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO)
+	else:
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, _facing.angle(), scale_factor)
+		_draw_body()
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO)
+	if _flash_material:
+		_flash_material.set_shader_parameter("hit", 1.0 if _flash > 0.0 else 0.0)
+
+
+func _draw_body() -> void:
+	var armor := Color(0.32, 0.34, 0.22) if phase == 1 else Color(0.53, 0.07, 0.065)
+	var light := Color(0.48, 0.48, 0.32) if phase == 1 else Color(0.76, 0.17, 0.13)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2.ZERO, 18.5, Color(0.10, 0.065, 0.055))
+	for side in [-1.0, 1.0]:
+		var step := sin(_walk / 7.0 * PI * 0.5 + side * PI * 0.5) * 2.0
+		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-9.0 + step, side * 13.0), 7.0, armor.darkened(0.3))
+		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(6.0, side * 12.0), 8.5, armor)
+		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(7.0, side * 11.0), 5.5, light)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-3.0, 0.0), 14.0, armor)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-5.0, -2.0), 9.0, light)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(15.0, 0.0), 8.0, Color(0.18, 0.075, 0.045))
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(18.0, 0.0), 5.0, light)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(19.0, -3.0), 1.5, Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.12))
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(19.0, 3.0), 1.5, Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.12))
+	if phase == 2:
+		for k in 4:
+			_body.draw_line(Vector2(-10.0 + k * 5.0, -5.0), Vector2(-7.0 + k * 5.0, 6.0), Color(0.22, 0.012, 0.012), 2.0)
+
+
+func _draw_attacks() -> void:
+	if _state == State.TELEGRAPH:
+		var t := clampf(_age / _telegraph_time, 0.0, 1.0)
+		# Интеграл растущей частоты: мигание ускоряется без скачков фазы.
+		var alpha := 0.55 + 0.35 * sin(TAU * (2.0 * t + 5.0 * t * t))
+		var a := to_local(_charge_from)
+		var b := to_local(_charge_target)
+		draw_line(a, b, Color(0.85, 0.025, 0.02, 0.1), RADIUS * 2.0)
+		draw_line(a, b, Color(1.0, 0.12, 0.08, alpha), 2.0)
+		draw_arc(b, 8.0, 0.0, TAU, 24, Color(1.0, 0.12, 0.08, alpha), 1.5)
+	elif _state == State.SLAM:
+		var p := to_local(_slam_pos)
+		if not _slam_wave:
+			draw_arc(p, SLAM_RADIUS, 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(1.0, 0.1, 0.05, 0.35 + 0.3 * _age / SLAM_WINDUP), 2.0)
+		else:
+			var t := clampf((_age - SLAM_WINDUP) / SLAM_TIME, 0.0, 1.0)
+			draw_arc(p, maxf(0.1, SLAM_RADIUS * t), 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(0.85, 0.09, 0.035, 0.5), SLAM_WIDTH)
+			draw_arc(p, maxf(0.1, SLAM_RADIUS * t), 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(1.0, 0.37, 0.18, 0.9), 2.0)
+	elif _state == State.ROAR:
+		for k in 3:
+			var t := fposmod(_age / ROAR_TIME + float(k) / 3.0, 1.0)
+			draw_arc(Vector2.ZERO, 20.0 + t * 100.0, 0.0, TAU, 48, Color(0.8, 0.25, 0.17, 0.5 * (1.0 - t)), 2.0)
diff --git a/tasks/inbox/G6/test_boss.gd b/tasks/inbox/G6/test_boss.gd

+extends SceneTree
+## Проверка в копии zera: --headless --disable-vsync --path . --script res://test_boss.gd
+
+const STEP := 1.0 / 60.0
+var world: Node2D
+var boss: Boss
+var player: Player
+var horde: Horde
+var effects: Effects
+var clock := 0.0
+var failures := 0
+var telegraphs := 0
+var slams := 0
+var roars := 0
+var crushes := 0
+var plates := 0
+var deaths := 0
+var charge_begin := 0.0
+var charge_wait := 0.0
+var previous_state := -1
+var physics_usec := 0
+var physics_max := 0
+var samples := 0
+var damage_values: Array[float] = []
+
+
+func _initialize() -> void:
+	call_deferred("_run")
+
+
+func _check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
+	if not ok:
+		failures += 1
+		print("FAIL ", label)
+
+
+func _run() -> void:
+	seed(706)
+	world = Node2D.new()
+	world.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_DISABLED
+	root.add_child(world)
+	player = Player.new()
+	var sprite := Sprite2D.new()
+	sprite.name = "Sprite"
+	player.add_child(sprite)
+	player.max_hp = 1000000.0
+	world.add_child(player)
+	player.hurt.connect(func(amount: float) -> void: damage_values.append(amount))
+	effects = Effects.new()
+	world.add_child(effects)
+	horde = Horde.new()
+	world.add_child(horde)
+	horde.setup(Rect2(0.0, 0.0, 640.0, 360.0), player, null)
+	for i in 200:
+		horde.spawn(Vector2(randf_range(90.0, 550.0), randf_range(140.0, 220.0)))
+	boss = Boss.new()
+	boss.player = player
+	boss.horde = horde
+	boss.effects = effects
+	boss.arena = horde.arena
+	world.add_child(boss)
+	boss.charge_telegraph.connect(_telegraph)
+	boss.slam_started.connect(func(_p: Vector2) -> void:
+		slams += 1
+		print("EVENT t=%.3f slam phase=%d" % [clock, boss.phase]))
+	boss.roar.connect(func(count: int) -> void:
+		roars += 1
+		print("EVENT t=%.3f roar count=%d" % [clock, count])
+		for i in count:
+			horde.spawn(boss.global_position + Vector2.from_angle(float(i) * TAU / count) * 65.0, Horde.Kind.RUNNER))
+	boss.crushed.connect(func(_p: Vector2, _dir: Vector2) -> void: crushes += 1)
+	boss.armor_plate.connect(func(_p: Vector2, _dir: Vector2) -> void: plates += 1)
+	boss.phase_changed.connect(func(phase: int) -> void: print("EVENT t=%.3f phase=%d hp=%.2f plates=%d" % [clock, phase, boss.hp, plates]))
+	boss.died.connect(func(_p: Vector2) -> void: deaths += 1)
+	boss.start(Vector2(80.0, 180.0))
+	print("SIM begin seconds=60 hz=60 shamblers=200 seed=706")
+	for frame in 3600:
+		clock = float(frame + 1) * STEP
+		if frame == 1800:
+			boss.take_damage(15999.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.global_position)
+			_check(boss.phase == 1 and is_equal_approx(boss.hp, 12000.75), "phase stays 1 above 50%")
+			boss.take_damage(1.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.global_position)
+			_check(boss.phase == 2 and is_equal_approx(boss.hp, 12000.0), "phase 2 at exactly 50%")
+			var hp_before := boss.hp
+			boss.take_damage(999.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.global_position)
+			_check(boss.hp == hp_before, "armor shedding invulnerability")
+		var segment := int(clock / 8.0) % 2
+		if segment == 0:
+			player.global_position = Vector2(320.0, 180.0) + Vector2.from_angle(clock * 0.4) * 150.0
+		else:
+			player.global_position = boss.global_position.move_toward(Vector2(320.0, 180.0), 75.0)
+		player._iframes = maxf(player._iframes - STEP, 0.0)
+		horde._physics_process(STEP)
+		_tick(STEP)
+		effects._physics_process(STEP)
+		_check(boss.global_position == boss._bounded(boss.global_position), "arena containment")
+	_check(telegraphs >= 5 and slams > 1 and roars > 0 and crushes > 0, "all attacks and crushing in 60 s")
+	_check(boss.phase == 2 and effects.particle_count() > 0, "phase 2 blood")
+	print("SIM end telegraphs=%d slams=%d roars=%d crushed=%d plates=%d" % [telegraphs, slams, roars, crushes, plates])
+	print("PERF boss_physics_avg_ms=%.6f max_ms=%.6f samples=%d" % [float(physics_usec) / samples / 1000.0, float(physics_max) / 1000.0, samples])
+	_check(float(physics_usec) / samples < 300.0, "boss average < 0.3 ms")
+	_unit_checks()
+	# Настоящая пауза дерева: прямые вызовы выше используются только для ускорения.
+	world.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_INHERIT
+	player.set_physics_process(false)
+	horde.set_physics_process(false)
+	effects.set_physics_process(false)
+	boss.start(Vector2(200.0, 180.0))
+	paused = true
+	var old_age := boss._age
+	for i in 3:
+		await physics_frame
+	_check(boss._age == old_age, "scene pause")
+	paused = false
+	print("CHECK pause PASS" if boss._age == old_age else "CHECK pause FAIL")
+	print("RESULT failures=%d" % failures)
+	quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
+
+
+func _telegraph(_from: Vector2, _to: Vector2, duration: float) -> void:
+	telegraphs += 1
+	charge_begin = clock
+	charge_wait = duration
+	print("EVENT t=%.3f telegraph phase=%d duration=%.1f" % [clock, boss.phase, duration])
+
+
+func _tick(delta: float) -> void:
+	var before := Time.get_ticks_usec()
+	boss._physics_process(delta)
+	var elapsed := Time.get_ticks_usec() - before
+	physics_usec += elapsed
+	physics_max = maxi(physics_max, elapsed)
+	samples += 1
+	if boss._state == Boss.State.CHARGE and previous_state == Boss.State.TELEGRAPH:
+		_check(clock - charge_begin + 0.00001 >= charge_wait, "full charge telegraph")
+	previous_state = boss._state
+
+
+func _reset(p := Vector2(200.0, 180.0)) -> void:
+	boss.start(p)
+	boss.horde = null
+	damage_values.clear()
+	player._iframes = 0.0
+	player.is_dead = false
+	player.global_position = boss.global_position
+
+
+func _unit_checks() -> void:
+	_reset()
+	boss._charge_cd = 99.0
+	boss._slam_cd = 99.0
+	boss._roar_cd = 99.0
+	for i in 49:
+		player._iframes = 0.0
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values == [20.0, 20.0], "contact 20, cooldown 0.8")
+	_reset()
+	boss.take_damage(16000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	boss._physics_process(1.2)
+	boss._charge_cd = 99.0
+	boss._slam_cd = 99.0
+	boss._roar_cd = 99.0
+	boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values == [25.0], "phase 2 contact 25")
+	print("CHECK contact phase1=20 phase2=25 cooldown=0.8 PASS")
+	_reset(Vector2(100.0, 180.0))
+	player.position = Vector2(130.0, 180.0)
+	boss._charges_left = 1
+	boss._begin_telegraph()
+	for i in 60:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values.is_empty(), "charge has no windup damage")
+	boss._physics_process(0.25)
+	boss._physics_process(0.25)
+	_check(damage_values == [35.0], "swept charge 35 once")
+	print("CHECK charge telegraph=1.0 swept_damage=35 once PASS")
+	_reset()
+	player.position += Vector2(80.0, 0.0)
+	boss._choose_attack()
+	for i in 29:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values.is_empty() and not boss._slam_wave, "slam windup no damage")
+	for i in 38:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values == [25.0], "slam ring 25 once")
+	_reset()
+	player.position += Vector2(80.0, 0.0)
+	boss._choose_attack()
+	player._iframes = 10.0
+	for i in 67:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values.is_empty(), "slam dash invulnerability")
+	print("CHECK slam windup=0.5 radius=150 time=0.6 width=20 damage=25 once dash_safe PASS")
+	_reset()
+	boss.take_damage(16000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	boss._physics_process(1.2)
+	player.position = Vector2(500.0, 180.0)
+	var t: Array[float] = []
+	var record := func(_a: Vector2, _b: Vector2, duration: float) -> void: t.append(duration)
+	boss.charge_telegraph.connect(record)
+	boss._choose_attack()
+	var recover_frames := 0
+	var recovery_lengths: Array[int] = []
+	var last_state := boss._state
+	for i in 2000:
+		if boss._state == Boss.State.RECOVER:
+			recover_frames += 1
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+		if last_state == Boss.State.RECOVER and boss._state != Boss.State.RECOVER:
+			recovery_lengths.append(recover_frames)
+			recover_frames = 0
+		last_state = boss._state
+		player.position = Vector2(640.0, 360.0) - player.position
+		if recovery_lengths.size() == 3:
+			break
+	boss.charge_telegraph.disconnect(record)
+	_check(t == [0.7, 0.7, 0.7], "three fresh phase 2 telegraphs")
+	_check(recovery_lengths == [24, 24, 36], "recover 0.4/0.4/0.6")
+	print("CHECK phase2 telegraphs=%s recovery_frames=%s" % [t, recovery_lengths])
+	_reset(Vector2(622.0, 342.0))
+	player.position = Vector2(639.0, 359.0)
+	boss._charges_left = 1
+	boss._begin_telegraph()
+	boss._physics_process(1.0)
+	boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(boss._state == Boss.State.RECOVER, "wall ends charge")
+	boss.global_position = Vector2(-999.0, 999.0)
+	boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(boss.position == Vector2(18.0, 342.0), "external displacement clamped")
+	_check(boss.hit_test(boss.position + Vector2(20.0, 0.0), 2.0), "hit test tangent")
+	_reset()
+	var before := deaths
+	_check(boss.take_damage(100000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position), "lethal returns true")
+	_check(not boss.is_alive() and not boss.visible and deaths == before + 1, "death hides and emits once")
+	_check(not boss.take_damage(10.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position) and deaths == before + 1, "no repeated death")
+	_check(not boss.hit_test(boss.position, 10.0), "dead hit test false")
+	print("CHECK wall_recover displacement hit_test death PASS")

 succeeded in 22ms:
class_name Boss
extends Node2D
## «Объект-7»: отдельный босс, без физических тел и нод на атаки.

const RADIUS := 18.0
const MAX_HP := 24000.0
const CHARGE_SPEED := 400.0
const CHARGE_COOLDOWN := 6.0
const SLAM_COOLDOWN := 9.0
const ROAR_COOLDOWN := 15.0
const SLAM_WINDUP := 0.5
const SLAM_TIME := 0.6
const SLAM_RADIUS := 150.0
const SLAM_WIDTH := 20.0
const ROAR_TIME := 0.8
const SHED_TIME := 1.2
const CELL := Vector2(144.0, 144.0)

signal phase_changed(phase: int)
signal died(pos: Vector2)
signal charge_telegraph(from: Vector2, to: Vector2, time: float)
signal slam_started(pos: Vector2)
signal roar(count: int)
signal crushed(pos: Vector2, dir: Vector2)
signal armor_plate(pos: Vector2, dir: Vector2)

var horde: Horde
var player: Player
var effects: Effects
var floor_decals: FloorDecals
var camera: GameCamera
var arena: Rect2
var minutes := 0.0

var hp := MAX_HP
var phase := 1

enum State { WALK, TELEGRAPH, CHARGE, RECOVER, SLAM, ROAR, SHED }

var _alive := false
var _state := State.WALK
var _last_attack := -1
var _age := 0.0
var _time := 0.0
var _walk := 0.0
var _facing := Vector2.RIGHT
var _charge_cd := 0.0
var _slam_cd := 0.0
var _roar_cd := 0.0
var _contact_cd := 0.0
var _flash := 0.0
var _plate_damage := 0.0
var _blood_time := 0.0
var _charges_left := 0
var _charge_target := Vector2.ZERO
var _charge_from := Vector2.ZERO
var _charge_hit := false
var _telegraph_time := 1.0
var _recover_time := 0.6
var _slam_pos := Vector2.ZERO
var _slam_hit := false
var _slam_wave := false
var _wave_previous := 0.0
var _sheet: Texture2D
var _flash_material: ShaderMaterial
var _body: Node2D


func _ready() -> void:
	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://art/boss.png"):
		_sheet = PixelArt.tex("boss")
	# Один слой тела отделяет белую вспышку от телеграфов и тени.
	var shader := Shader.new()
	shader.code = "shader_type canvas_item; uniform float hit = 0.0; void fragment() { vec4 c = texture(TEXTURE, UV) * COLOR; COLOR = vec4(mix(c.rgb, vec3(1.0), hit), c.a); }"
	_flash_material = ShaderMaterial.new()
	_flash_material.shader = shader
	_body = Node2D.new()
	_body.material = _flash_material
	_body.draw.connect(_draw_sprite)
	add_child(_body)
	visible = _alive


func start(p: Vector2) -> void:
	hp = MAX_HP
	phase = 1
	_alive = true
	_state = State.WALK
	_last_attack = -1
	_age = 0.0
	_time = 0.0
	_walk = 0.0
	_facing = Vector2.RIGHT
	_charge_cd = 0.0
	_slam_cd = 0.0
	_roar_cd = 0.0
	_contact_cd = 0.0
	_flash = 0.0
	_plate_damage = 0.0
	_blood_time = 0.0
	_charges_left = 0
	_charge_hit = false
	_slam_hit = false
	_slam_wave = false
	global_position = _bounded(p)
	visible = true
	queue_redraw()


func take_damage(amount: float, dir: Vector2, at: Vector2) -> bool:
	if not _alive or _state == State.SHED or amount <= 0.0:
		return false
	var damage := amount * (0.75 if phase == 1 else 1.0)
	hp = maxf(hp - damage, 0.0)
	_flash = 0.08
	if hp <= 0.0:
		_alive = false
		visible = false
		_charges_left = 0
		_slam_wave = false
		died.emit(global_position)
		return true
	if phase == 1:
		_plate_damage += damage
		while _plate_damage >= 1500.0:
			_plate_damage -= 1500.0
			armor_plate.emit(at, dir.normalized() if dir != Vector2.ZERO else _facing)
		if hp <= MAX_HP * 0.5:
			_shed_armor()
	elif effects:
		effects.blood_hit(at, dir, false)
	queue_redraw()
	return false


func hit_test(p: Vector2, r: float) -> bool:
	return _alive and p.distance_squared_to(global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + maxf(r, 0.0), 2.0)


func is_alive() -> bool:
	return _alive


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not _alive:
		return
	global_position = _bounded(global_position)
	_time += delta
	_age += delta
	_flash = maxf(_flash - delta, 0.0)
	_charge_cd = maxf(_charge_cd - delta, 0.0)
	_slam_cd = maxf(_slam_cd - delta, 0.0)
	_roar_cd = maxf(_roar_cd - delta, 0.0)
	_contact_cd = maxf(_contact_cd - delta, 0.0)
	if _state == State.SHED:
		if _age + 0.000001 >= SHED_TIME:
			_enter(State.WALK)
	elif player and not player.is_dead:
		match _state:
			State.WALK:
				_choose_attack()
				if _state == State.WALK:
					_move_to(global_position.move_toward(player.global_position, (40.0 if phase == 1 else 60.0) * delta))
					_contact()
			State.TELEGRAPH:
				if _age + 0.000001 >= _telegraph_time:
					_charge_hit = false
					_enter(State.CHARGE)
			State.CHARGE:
				_charge(delta)
			State.RECOVER:
				if _age + 0.000001 >= _recover_time:
					if _charges_left > 0:
						_begin_telegraph()
					else:
						_enter(State.WALK)
			State.SLAM:
				_slam()
			State.ROAR:
				if _age + 0.000001 >= ROAR_TIME:
					_roar_cd = ROAR_COOLDOWN
					_enter(State.WALK)
					roar.emit(15 if phase == 1 else 25)
	var moving := _state == State.WALK or _state == State.CHARGE
	if phase == 2 and moving and effects:
		_blood_time += delta
		if _blood_time >= 0.1:
			_blood_time = 0.0
			effects.emit(global_position + Vector2.from_angle(randf() * TAU) * 10.0, -_facing * 12.0, 0.3, Effects.BLOOD, 2.5, 5.0, true)
	queue_redraw()


func _choose_attack() -> void:
	var distance := global_position.distance_to(player.global_position)
	if distance > 180.0 and _charge_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.CHARGE:
		_begin_charge_series()
	elif distance < 120.0 and _slam_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.SLAM:
		_last_attack = State.SLAM
		_slam_pos = global_position
		_slam_hit = false
		_slam_wave = false
		_enter(State.SLAM)
	elif _roar_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.ROAR and _few_runners():
		_last_attack = State.ROAR
		_enter(State.ROAR)
		if effects:
			effects.blood_burst(global_position + _facing * 18.0, _facing, 18, 80.0, 0.8)
	elif _charge_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.CHARGE and distance > RADIUS + Player.RADIUS:
		# Дистанция задаёт приоритет, но не блокирует чередование при живых бегунах.
		_begin_charge_series()


func _begin_charge_series() -> void:
	_last_attack = State.CHARGE
	_charges_left = 1 if phase == 1 else 3
	_begin_telegraph()


func _few_runners() -> bool:
	if horde == null:
		return true
	var runners := 0
	for j in horde.query_radius(global_position, 260.0):
		if j >= 0 and j < horde.count and horde.dead[j] == 0 and horde.kind[j] == Horde.Kind.RUNNER:
			runners += 1
			if runners >= 8:
				return false
	return true


func _begin_telegraph() -> void:
	var offset := player.global_position - global_position
	if offset.length_squared() > 0.000001:
		_facing = offset.normalized()
	_charge_from = global_position
	# Луч до внутренней границы: вся туша остаётся в арене.
	var inner := _inner_arena()
	var distance := 100000.0
	if absf(_facing.x) > 0.000001:
		var edge := inner.end.x if _facing.x > 0.0 else inner.position.x
		distance = minf(distance, maxf((edge - global_position.x) / _facing.x, 0.0))
	if absf(_facing.y) > 0.000001:
		var edge := inner.end.y if _facing.y > 0.0 else inner.position.y
		distance = minf(distance, maxf((edge - global_position.y) / _facing.y, 0.0))
	_charge_target = _bounded(global_position + _facing * distance)
	_telegraph_time = 1.0 if phase == 1 else 0.7
	_enter(State.TELEGRAPH)
	charge_telegraph.emit(_charge_from, _charge_target, _telegraph_time)


func _charge(delta: float) -> void:
	var from := global_position
	_move_to(from.move_toward(_charge_target, CHARGE_SPEED * delta))
	var to := global_position
	if not _charge_hit:
		var nearest := Geometry2D.get_closest_point_to_segment(player.global_position, from, to)
		if nearest.distance_squared_to(player.global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + Player.RADIUS, 2.0):
			_charge_hit = true # один запрос урона на проход, даже при неуязвимости
			player.take_damage(35.0)
	if not _alive or _state != State.CHARGE:
		return
	if horde:
		var middle := (from + to) * 0.5
		for j in horde.query_radius(middle, from.distance_to(to) * 0.5 + RADIUS):
			if j < 0 or j >= horde.count or horde.dead[j] != 0:
				continue
			var p := horde.pos[j]
			var nearest := Geometry2D.get_closest_point_to_segment(p, from, to)
			if nearest.distance_squared_to(p) <= pow(RADIUS + horde.radius[j], 2.0):
				if horde.damage(j, 9999.0, _facing, 300.0, WeaponDefs.Ammo.STANDARD, false, horde.pos[j], true):
					crushed.emit(p, _facing)
				if not _alive or _state != State.CHARGE:
					return
	if to.distance_squared_to(_charge_target) < 0.000001 or to == from:
		_charges_left -= 1
		_recover_time = 0.4 if _charges_left > 0 else 0.6
		if _charges_left == 0:
			_charge_cd = CHARGE_COOLDOWN
		_enter(State.RECOVER)


func _slam() -> void:
	if _age + 0.000001 < SLAM_WINDUP:
		return
	if not _slam_wave:
		_slam_wave = true
		_slam_cd = SLAM_COOLDOWN * (0.7 if phase == 2 else 1.0)
		if camera:
			camera.shake(6.0)
		slam_started.emit(_slam_pos)
		if not _alive or _state != State.SLAM:
			return
	var radius := SLAM_RADIUS * clampf((_age - SLAM_WINDUP) / SLAM_TIME, 0.0, 1.0)
	# Относительное движение игрока и фронта волны между двумя тиками.
	var relative := player.global_position.distance_to(_slam_pos) - radius
	if not _slam_hit:
		if absf(relative) <= SLAM_WIDTH * 0.5 + Player.RADIUS or _wave_previous * relative < 0.0:
			_slam_hit = true
			player.take_damage(25.0)
	_wave_previous = relative
	if _age + 0.000001 >= SLAM_WINDUP + SLAM_TIME:
		_slam_wave = false
		_enter(State.WALK)


func _contact() -> void:
	if _contact_cd <= 0.0 and global_position.distance_squared_to(player.global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + Player.RADIUS, 2.0):
		_contact_cd = 0.8
		player.take_damage(20.0 if phase == 1 else 25.0)


func _shed_armor() -> void:
	phase = 2
	_charges_left = 0
	_slam_wave = false
	_enter(State.SHED)
	if camera:
		camera.shake(9.0)
	if effects:
		effects.blood_burst(global_position, _facing, 100, 190.0, PI)
	for k in 8:
		var dir := Vector2.from_angle(float(k) * TAU / 8.0)
		armor_plate.emit(global_position + dir * 12.0, dir)
	phase_changed.emit(2)


func _enter(state: State) -> void:
	_state = state
	_age = 0.0
	if state == State.SLAM:
		_wave_previous = player.global_position.distance_to(global_position)


func _move_to(p: Vector2) -> void:
	var before := global_position
	global_position = _bounded(p)
	var step := global_position - before
	_walk += step.length()
	if step.length_squared() > 0.000001:
		_facing = step.normalized()


func _inner_arena() -> Rect2:
	var margin := Vector2(minf(RADIUS, maxf(arena.size.x, 0.0) * 0.5), minf(RADIUS, maxf(arena.size.y, 0.0) * 0.5))
	return Rect2(arena.position + margin, (arena.size - margin * 2.0).max(Vector2.ZERO))


func _bounded(p: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	var inner := _inner_arena()
	return p.clamp(inner.position, inner.end)


func _draw() -> void:
	if not _alive:
		return
	_draw_attacks()
	PixelArt.draw_dot(self, Vector2(1.0, 4.0), 20.0, Color(0.015, 0.012, 0.012, 0.55))
	if _body:
		_body.queue_redraw()


func _draw_sprite() -> void:
	var breath := 1.0 + 0.035 * sin(_time * (9.0 if _state == State.RECOVER else 3.0))
	var scale_factor := Vector2(breath, 1.0 / breath)
	if _state == State.SLAM and not _slam_wave:
		scale_factor *= Vector2(1.12, 0.8)
	if _state == State.SHED:
		scale_factor *= 1.0 + 0.07 * sin(_age * 45.0)
	if _sheet:
		var col := 4 if _state == State.CHARGE else int(_walk / 7.0) % 4
		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, _facing.angle(), scale_factor * PixelArt.ART_SCALE)
		_body.draw_texture_rect_region(_sheet, Rect2(-CELL * 0.5, CELL), Rect2(Vector2(col, phase - 1) * CELL, CELL))
		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO)
	else:
		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, _facing.angle(), scale_factor)
		_draw_body()
		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO)
	if _flash_material:
		_flash_material.set_shader_parameter("hit", 1.0 if _flash > 0.0 else 0.0)


func _draw_body() -> void:
	var armor := Color(0.32, 0.34, 0.22) if phase == 1 else Color(0.53, 0.07, 0.065)
	var light := Color(0.48, 0.48, 0.32) if phase == 1 else Color(0.76, 0.17, 0.13)
	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2.ZERO, 18.5, Color(0.10, 0.065, 0.055))
	for side in [-1.0, 1.0]:
		var step := sin(_walk / 7.0 * PI * 0.5 + side * PI * 0.5) * 2.0
		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-9.0 + step, side * 13.0), 7.0, armor.darkened(0.3))
		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(6.0, side * 12.0), 8.5, armor)
		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(7.0, side * 11.0), 5.5, light)
	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-3.0, 0.0), 14.0, armor)
	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-5.0, -2.0), 9.0, light)
	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(15.0, 0.0), 8.0, Color(0.18, 0.075, 0.045))
	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(18.0, 0.0), 5.0, light)
	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(19.0, -3.0), 1.5, Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.12))
	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(19.0, 3.0), 1.5, Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.12))
	if phase == 2:
		for k in 4:
			_body.draw_line(Vector2(-10.0 + k * 5.0, -5.0), Vector2(-7.0 + k * 5.0, 6.0), Color(0.22, 0.012, 0.012), 2.0)


func _draw_attacks() -> void:
	if _state == State.TELEGRAPH:
		var t := clampf(_age / _telegraph_time, 0.0, 1.0)
		# Интеграл растущей частоты: мигание ускоряется без скачков фазы.
		var alpha := 0.55 + 0.35 * sin(TAU * (2.0 * t + 5.0 * t * t))
		var a := to_local(_charge_from)
		var b := to_local(_charge_target)
		draw_line(a, b, Color(0.85, 0.025, 0.02, 0.1), RADIUS * 2.0)
		draw_line(a, b, Color(1.0, 0.12, 0.08, alpha), 2.0)
		draw_arc(b, 8.0, 0.0, TAU, 24, Color(1.0, 0.12, 0.08, alpha), 1.5)
	elif _state == State.SLAM:
		var p := to_local(_slam_pos)
		if not _slam_wave:
			draw_arc(p, SLAM_RADIUS, 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(1.0, 0.1, 0.05, 0.35 + 0.3 * _age / SLAM_WINDUP), 2.0)
		else:
			var t := clampf((_age - SLAM_WINDUP) / SLAM_TIME, 0.0, 1.0)
			draw_arc(p, maxf(0.1, SLAM_RADIUS * t), 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(0.85, 0.09, 0.035, 0.5), SLAM_WIDTH)
			draw_arc(p, maxf(0.1, SLAM_RADIUS * t), 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(1.0, 0.37, 0.18, 0.9), 2.0)
	elif _state == State.ROAR:
		for k in 3:
			var t := fposmod(_age / ROAR_TIME + float(k) / 3.0, 1.0)
			draw_arc(Vector2.ZERO, 20.0 + t * 100.0, 0.0, TAU, 48, Color(0.8, 0.25, 0.17, 0.5 * (1.0 - t)), 2.0)

+extends SceneTree
+
+var screen: Node2D
+var panels: Array[SubViewport] = []
+
+
+func _initialize() -> void:
+	call_deferred("_run")
+
+
+func _run() -> void:
+	root.size = Vector2i(1280, 720)
+	screen = Node2D.new()
+	root.add_child(screen)
+	var captions := ["PHASE 1 / ARMOR", "PHASE 2 / EXPOSED", "CHARGE / TELEGRAPH", "SLAM / WINDUP", "SLAM / WAVE", "ROAR / DISTORTION"]
+	for i in 6:
+		var viewport := SubViewport.new()
+		viewport.size = Vector2i(426, 360)
+		viewport.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_ALWAYS
+		root.add_child(viewport)
+		panels.append(viewport)
+		var back := Node2D.new()
+		viewport.add_child(back)
+		back.draw.connect(func() -> void:
+			back.draw_rect(Rect2(0, 0, 426, 360), Color(0.12, 0.135, 0.12))
+			for x in range(0, 426, 24):
+				back.draw_line(Vector2(x, 40), Vector2(x, 360), Color(0.16, 0.17, 0.15))
+			for y in range(48, 360, 24):
+				back.draw_line(Vector2(0, y), Vector2(426, y), Color(0.16, 0.17, 0.15))
+			back.draw_rect(Rect2(0, 0, 426, 40), Color(0.045, 0.055, 0.05))
+			back.draw_string(ThemeDB.fallback_font, Vector2(16, 27), captions[i], HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 18, Color(0.85, 0.86, 0.77)))
+		var boss := Boss.new()
+		boss.arena = Rect2(0, 40, 426, 320)
+		viewport.add_child(boss)
+		boss.start(Vector2(213, 200))
+		boss.set_physics_process(false)
+		if i <= 1:
+			boss.scale = Vector2.ONE * 3.0
+		if i == 1:
+			boss.phase = 2
+		if i == 2:
+			boss._state = Boss.State.TELEGRAPH
+			boss._age = 0.78
+			boss._charge_from = boss.position
+			boss._charge_target = Vector2(408, 120)
+			boss._facing = (boss._charge_target - boss.position).normalized()
+		if i == 3 or i == 4:
+			boss._state = Boss.State.SLAM
+			boss._slam_pos = boss.position
+			boss._age = 0.35 if i == 3 else 0.96
+			boss._slam_wave = i == 4
+		if i == 5:
+			boss.phase = 2
+			boss._state = Boss.State.ROAR
+			boss._age = 0.5
+		boss.queue_redraw()
+		var view := Sprite2D.new()
+		view.centered = false
+		view.texture = viewport.get_texture()
+		view.position = Vector2((i % 3) * 427, (i / 3) * 360)
+		screen.add_child(view)
+	for frame in 8:
+		await process_frame
+	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
+	var image := root.get_texture().get_image()
+	var error := image.save_png(OS.get_cmdline_user_args()[0])
+	print("PREVIEW save_png=", error, " size=", image.get_size())
+	quit(error)

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..f047c33c46f4327acab1f528c02ffe794dacde44

+++ b/tasks/inbox/G6/boss.gd
@@ -0,0 +1,423 @@
+class_name Boss
+extends Node2D
+## «Объект-7»: отдельный босс, без физических тел и нод на атаки.
+
+const RADIUS := 18.0
+const MAX_HP := 24000.0
+const CHARGE_SPEED := 400.0
+const CHARGE_COOLDOWN := 6.0
+const SLAM_COOLDOWN := 9.0
+const ROAR_COOLDOWN := 15.0
+const SLAM_WINDUP := 0.5
+const SLAM_TIME := 0.6
+const SLAM_RADIUS := 150.0
+const SLAM_WIDTH := 20.0
+const ROAR_TIME := 0.8
+const SHED_TIME := 1.2
+const CELL := Vector2(144.0, 144.0)
+
+signal phase_changed(phase: int)
+signal died(pos: Vector2)
+signal charge_telegraph(from: Vector2, to: Vector2, time: float)
+signal slam_started(pos: Vector2)
+signal roar(count: int)
+signal crushed(pos: Vector2, dir: Vector2)
+signal armor_plate(pos: Vector2, dir: Vector2)
+
+var horde: Horde
+var player: Player
+var effects: Effects
+var floor_decals: FloorDecals
+var camera: GameCamera
+var arena: Rect2
+var minutes := 0.0
+
+var hp := MAX_HP
+var phase := 1
+
+enum State { WALK, TELEGRAPH, CHARGE, RECOVER, SLAM, ROAR, SHED }
+
+var _alive := false
+var _state := State.WALK
+var _last_attack := -1
+var _age := 0.0
+var _time := 0.0
+var _walk := 0.0
+var _facing := Vector2.RIGHT
+var _charge_cd := 0.0
+var _slam_cd := 0.0
+var _roar_cd := 0.0
+var _contact_cd := 0.0
+var _flash := 0.0
+var _plate_damage := 0.0
+var _blood_time := 0.0
+var _charges_left := 0
+var _charge_target := Vector2.ZERO
+var _charge_from := Vector2.ZERO
+var _charge_hit := false
+var _telegraph_time := 1.0
+var _recover_time := 0.6
+var _slam_pos := Vector2.ZERO
+var _slam_hit := false
+var _slam_wave := false
+var _wave_previous := 0.0
+var _sheet: Texture2D
+var _flash_material: ShaderMaterial
+var _body: Node2D
+
+
+func _ready() -> void:
+	if ResourceLoader.exists("res://art/boss.png"):
+		_sheet = PixelArt.tex("boss")
+	# Один слой тела отделяет белую вспышку от телеграфов и тени.
+	var shader := Shader.new()
+	shader.code = "shader_type canvas_item; uniform float hit = 0.0; void fragment() { COLOR.rgb = mix(COLOR.rgb, vec3(1.0), hit); }"
+	_flash_material = ShaderMaterial.new()
+	_flash_material.shader = shader
+	_body = Node2D.new()
+	_body.material = _flash_material
+	_body.draw.connect(_draw_sprite)
+	add_child(_body)
+	visible = _alive
+
+
+func start(p: Vector2) -> void:
+	hp = MAX_HP
+	phase = 1
+	_alive = true
+	_state = State.WALK
+	_last_attack = -1
+	_age = 0.0
+	_time = 0.0
+	_walk = 0.0
+	_facing = Vector2.RIGHT
+	_charge_cd = 0.0
+	_slam_cd = 0.0
+	_roar_cd = 0.0
+	_contact_cd = 0.0
+	_flash = 0.0
+	_plate_damage = 0.0
+	_blood_time = 0.0
+	_charges_left = 0
+	_charge_hit = false
+	_slam_hit = false
+	_slam_wave = false
+	global_position = _bounded(p)
+	visible = true
+	queue_redraw()
+
+
+func take_damage(amount: float, dir: Vector2, at: Vector2) -> bool:
+	if not _alive or _state == State.SHED or amount <= 0.0:
+		return false
+	var damage := amount * (0.75 if phase == 1 else 1.0)
+	hp = maxf(hp - damage, 0.0)
+	_flash = 0.08
+	if hp <= 0.0:
+		_alive = false
+		visible = false
+		_charges_left = 0
+		_slam_wave = false
+		died.emit(global_position)
+		return true
+	if phase == 1:
+		_plate_damage += damage
+		while _plate_damage >= 1500.0:
+			_plate_damage -= 1500.0
+			armor_plate.emit(at, dir.normalized() if dir != Vector2.ZERO else _facing)
+		if hp <= MAX_HP * 0.5:
+			_shed_armor()
+	elif effects:
+		effects.blood_hit(at, dir, false)
+	queue_redraw()
+	return false
+
+
+func hit_test(p: Vector2, r: float) -> bool:
+	return _alive and p.distance_squared_to(global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + maxf(r, 0.0), 2.0)
+
+
+func is_alive() -> bool:
+	return _alive
+
+
+func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
+	if not _alive:
+		return
+	global_position = _bounded(global_position)
+	_time += delta
+	_age += delta
+	_flash = maxf(_flash - delta, 0.0)
+	_charge_cd = maxf(_charge_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	_slam_cd = maxf(_slam_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	_roar_cd = maxf(_roar_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	_contact_cd = maxf(_contact_cd - delta, 0.0)
+	if _state == State.SHED:
+		if _age + 0.000001 >= SHED_TIME:
+			_enter(State.WALK)
+	elif player and not player.is_dead:
+		match _state:
+			State.WALK:
+				_choose_attack()
+				if _state == State.WALK:
+					_move_to(global_position.move_toward(player.global_position, (40.0 if phase == 1 else 60.0) * delta))
+					_contact()
+			State.TELEGRAPH:
+				if _age + 0.000001 >= _telegraph_time:
+					_charge_hit = false
+					_enter(State.CHARGE)
+			State.CHARGE:
+				_charge(delta)
+			State.RECOVER:
+				if _age + 0.000001 >= _recover_time:
+					if _charges_left > 0:
+						_begin_telegraph()
+					else:
+						_enter(State.WALK)
+			State.SLAM:
+				_slam()
+			State.ROAR:
+				if _age + 0.000001 >= ROAR_TIME:
+					_roar_cd = ROAR_COOLDOWN
+					_enter(State.WALK)
+					roar.emit(15 if phase == 1 else 25)
+	var moving := _state == State.WALK or _state == State.CHARGE
+	if phase == 2 and moving and effects:
+		_blood_time += delta
+		if _blood_time >= 0.1:
+			_blood_time = 0.0
+			effects.emit(global_position + Vector2.from_angle(randf() * TAU) * 10.0, -_facing * 12.0, 0.3, Effects.BLOOD, 2.5, 5.0, true)
+	queue_redraw()
+
+
+func _choose_attack() -> void:
+	var distance := global_position.distance_to(player.global_position)
+	if distance > 180.0 and _charge_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.CHARGE:
+		_begin_charge_series()
+	elif distance < 120.0 and _slam_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.SLAM:
+		_last_attack = State.SLAM
+		_slam_pos = global_position
+		_slam_hit = false
+		_slam_wave = false
+		_enter(State.SLAM)
+	elif _roar_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.ROAR and _few_runners():
+		_last_attack = State.ROAR
+		_enter(State.ROAR)
+		if effects:
+			effects.blood_burst(global_position + _facing * 18.0, _facing, 18, 80.0, 0.8)
+	elif _charge_cd <= 0.0 and _last_attack != State.CHARGE and distance > RADIUS + Player.RADIUS:
+		# Дистанция задаёт приоритет, но не блокирует чередование при живых бегунах.
+		_begin_charge_series()
+
+
+func _begin_charge_series() -> void:
+	_last_attack = State.CHARGE
+	_charges_left = 1 if phase == 1 else 3
+	_begin_telegraph()
+
+
+func _few_runners() -> bool:
+	if horde == null:
+		return true
+	var runners := 0
+	for j in horde.query_radius(global_position, 260.0):
+		if j >= 0 and j < horde.count and horde.dead[j] == 0 and horde.kind[j] == Horde.Kind.RUNNER:
+			runners += 1
+			if runners >= 8:
+				return false
+	return true
+
+
+func _begin_telegraph() -> void:
+	var offset := player.global_position - global_position
+	if offset.length_squared() > 0.000001:
+		_facing = offset.normalized()
+	_charge_from = global_position
+	# Луч до внутренней границы: вся туша остаётся в арене.
+	var inner := _inner_arena()
+	var distance := 100000.0
+	if absf(_facing.x) > 0.000001:
+		var edge := inner.end.x if _facing.x > 0.0 else inner.position.x
+		distance = minf(distance, maxf((edge - global_position.x) / _facing.x, 0.0))
+	if absf(_facing.y) > 0.000001:
+		var edge := inner.end.y if _facing.y > 0.0 else inner.position.y
+		distance = minf(distance, maxf((edge - global_position.y) / _facing.y, 0.0))
+	_charge_target = _bounded(global_position + _facing * distance)
+	_telegraph_time = 1.0 if phase == 1 else 0.7
+	_enter(State.TELEGRAPH)
+	charge_telegraph.emit(_charge_from, _charge_target, _telegraph_time)
+
+
+func _charge(delta: float) -> void:
+	var from := global_position
+	_move_to(from.move_toward(_charge_target, CHARGE_SPEED * delta))
+	var to := global_position
+	if not _charge_hit:
+		var nearest := Geometry2D.get_closest_point_to_segment(player.global_position, from, to)
+		if nearest.distance_squared_to(player.global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + Player.RADIUS, 2.0):
+			_charge_hit = true # один запрос урона на проход, даже при неуязвимости
+			player.take_damage(35.0)
+	if not _alive or _state != State.CHARGE:
+		return
+	if horde:
+		var middle := (from + to) * 0.5
+		for j in horde.query_radius(middle, from.distance_to(to) * 0.5 + RADIUS):
+			if j < 0 or j >= horde.count or horde.dead[j] != 0:
+				continue
+			var p := horde.pos[j]
+			var nearest := Geometry2D.get_closest_point_to_segment(p, from, to)
+			if nearest.distance_squared_to(p) <= pow(RADIUS + horde.radius[j], 2.0):
+				if horde.damage(j, 9999.0, _facing, 300.0, WeaponDefs.Ammo.STANDARD, false, horde.pos[j], true):
+					crushed.emit(p, _facing)
+				if not _alive or _state != State.CHARGE:
+					return
+	if to.distance_squared_to(_charge_target) < 0.000001 or to == from:
+		_charges_left -= 1
+		_recover_time = 0.4 if _charges_left > 0 else 0.6
+		if _charges_left == 0:
+			_charge_cd = CHARGE_COOLDOWN
+		_enter(State.RECOVER)
+
+
+func _slam() -> void:
+	if _age + 0.000001 < SLAM_WINDUP:
+		return
+	if not _slam_wave:
+		_slam_wave = true
+		_slam_cd = SLAM_COOLDOWN * (0.7 if phase == 2 else 1.0)
+		if camera:
+			camera.shake(6.0)
+		slam_started.emit(_slam_pos)
+		if not _alive or _state != State.SLAM:
+			return
+	var radius := SLAM_RADIUS * clampf((_age - SLAM_WINDUP) / SLAM_TIME, 0.0, 1.0)
+	# Относительное движение игрока и фронта волны между двумя тиками.
+	var relative := player.global_position.distance_to(_slam_pos) - radius
+	if not _slam_hit:
+		if absf(relative) <= SLAM_WIDTH * 0.5 + Player.RADIUS or _wave_previous * relative < 0.0:
+			_slam_hit = true
+			player.take_damage(25.0)
+	_wave_previous = relative
+	if _age + 0.000001 >= SLAM_WINDUP + SLAM_TIME:
+		_slam_wave = false
+		_enter(State.WALK)
+
+
+func _contact() -> void:
+	if _contact_cd <= 0.0 and global_position.distance_squared_to(player.global_position) <= pow(RADIUS + Player.RADIUS, 2.0):
+		_contact_cd = 0.8
+		player.take_damage(20.0 if phase == 1 else 25.0)
+
+
+func _shed_armor() -> void:
+	phase = 2
+	_charges_left = 0
+	_slam_wave = false
+	_enter(State.SHED)
+	if camera:
+		camera.shake(9.0)
+	if effects:
+		effects.blood_burst(global_position, _facing, 100, 190.0, PI)
+	for k in 8:
+		var dir := Vector2.from_angle(float(k) * TAU / 8.0)
+		armor_plate.emit(global_position + dir * 12.0, dir)
+	phase_changed.emit(2)
+
+
+func _enter(state: State) -> void:
+	_state = state
+	_age = 0.0
+	if state == State.SLAM:
+		_wave_previous = player.global_position.distance_to(global_position)
+
+
+func _move_to(p: Vector2) -> void:
+	var before := global_position
+	global_position = _bounded(p)
+	var step := global_position - before
+	_walk += step.length()
+	if step.length_squared() > 0.000001:
+		_facing = step.normalized()
+
+
+func _inner_arena() -> Rect2:
+	var margin := Vector2(minf(RADIUS, maxf(arena.size.x, 0.0) * 0.5), minf(RADIUS, maxf(arena.size.y, 0.0) * 0.5))
+	return Rect2(arena.position + margin, (arena.size - margin * 2.0).max(Vector2.ZERO))
+
+
+func _bounded(p: Vector2) -> Vector2:
+	var inner := _inner_arena()
+	return p.clamp(inner.position, inner.end)
+
+
+func _draw() -> void:
+	if not _alive:
+		return
+	_draw_attacks()
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(self, Vector2(1.0, 4.0), 20.0, Color(0.015, 0.012, 0.012, 0.55))
+	if _body:
+		_body.queue_redraw()
+
+
+func _draw_sprite() -> void:
+	var breath := 1.0 + 0.035 * sin(_time * (9.0 if _state == State.RECOVER else 3.0))
+	var scale_factor := Vector2(breath, 1.0 / breath)
+	if _state == State.SLAM and not _slam_wave:
+		scale_factor *= Vector2(1.12, 0.8)
+	if _state == State.SHED:
+		scale_factor *= 1.0 + 0.07 * sin(_age * 45.0)
+	if _sheet:
+		var col := 4 if _state == State.CHARGE else int(_walk / 7.0) % 4
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, _facing.angle(), scale_factor * PixelArt.ART_SCALE)
+		_body.draw_texture_rect_region(_sheet, Rect2(-CELL * 0.5, CELL), Rect2(Vector2(col, phase - 1) * CELL, CELL))
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO)
+	else:
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, _facing.angle(), scale_factor)
+		_draw_body()
+		_body.draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO)
+	if _flash_material:
+		_flash_material.set_shader_parameter("hit", 1.0 if _flash > 0.0 else 0.0)
+
+
+func _draw_body() -> void:
+	var armor := Color(0.32, 0.34, 0.22) if phase == 1 else Color(0.53, 0.07, 0.065)
+	var light := Color(0.48, 0.48, 0.32) if phase == 1 else Color(0.76, 0.17, 0.13)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2.ZERO, 18.5, Color(0.10, 0.065, 0.055))
+	for side in [-1.0, 1.0]:
+		var step := sin(_walk / 7.0 * PI * 0.5 + side * PI * 0.5) * 2.0
+		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-9.0 + step, side * 13.0), 7.0, armor.darkened(0.3))
+		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(6.0, side * 12.0), 8.5, armor)
+		PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(7.0, side * 11.0), 5.5, light)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-3.0, 0.0), 14.0, armor)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(-5.0, -2.0), 9.0, light)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(15.0, 0.0), 8.0, Color(0.18, 0.075, 0.045))
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(18.0, 0.0), 5.0, light)
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(19.0, -3.0), 1.5, Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.12))
+	PixelArt.draw_dot(_body, Vector2(19.0, 3.0), 1.5, Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.12))
+	if phase == 2:
+		for k in 4:
+			_body.draw_line(Vector2(-10.0 + k * 5.0, -5.0), Vector2(-7.0 + k * 5.0, 6.0), Color(0.22, 0.012, 0.012), 2.0)
+
+
+func _draw_attacks() -> void:
+	if _state == State.TELEGRAPH:
+		var t := clampf(_age / _telegraph_time, 0.0, 1.0)
+		# Интеграл растущей частоты: мигание ускоряется без скачков фазы.
+		var alpha := 0.55 + 0.35 * sin(TAU * (2.0 * t + 5.0 * t * t))
+		var a := to_local(_charge_from)
+		var b := to_local(_charge_target)
+		draw_line(a, b, Color(0.85, 0.025, 0.02, 0.1), RADIUS * 2.0)
+		draw_line(a, b, Color(1.0, 0.12, 0.08, alpha), 2.0)
+		draw_arc(b, 8.0, 0.0, TAU, 24, Color(1.0, 0.12, 0.08, alpha), 1.5)
+	elif _state == State.SLAM:
+		var p := to_local(_slam_pos)
+		if not _slam_wave:
+			draw_arc(p, SLAM_RADIUS, 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(1.0, 0.1, 0.05, 0.35 + 0.3 * _age / SLAM_WINDUP), 2.0)
+		else:
+			var t := clampf((_age - SLAM_WINDUP) / SLAM_TIME, 0.0, 1.0)
+			draw_arc(p, maxf(0.1, SLAM_RADIUS * t), 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(0.85, 0.09, 0.035, 0.5), SLAM_WIDTH)
+			draw_arc(p, maxf(0.1, SLAM_RADIUS * t), 0.0, TAU, 64, Color(1.0, 0.37, 0.18, 0.9), 2.0)
+	elif _state == State.ROAR:
+		for k in 3:
+			var t := fposmod(_age / ROAR_TIME + float(k) / 3.0, 1.0)
+			draw_arc(Vector2.ZERO, 20.0 + t * 100.0, 0.0, TAU, 48, Color(0.8, 0.25, 0.17, 0.5 * (1.0 - t)), 2.0)
diff --git a/tasks/inbox/G6/test_boss.gd b/tasks/inbox/G6/test_boss.gd

@@ -0,0 +1,269 @@
+extends SceneTree
+## Проверка в копии zera: --headless --disable-vsync --path . --script res://test_boss.gd
+
+const STEP := 1.0 / 60.0
+var world: Node2D
+var boss: Boss
+var player: Player
+var horde: Horde
+var effects: Effects
+var clock := 0.0
+var failures := 0
+var telegraphs := 0
+var slams := 0
+var roars := 0
+var crushes := 0
+var plates := 0
+var deaths := 0
+var charge_begin := 0.0
+var charge_wait := 0.0
+var previous_state := -1
+var physics_usec := 0
+var physics_max := 0
+var samples := 0
+var damage_values: Array[float] = []
+
+
+func _initialize() -> void:
+	call_deferred("_run")
+
+
+func _check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
+	if not ok:
+		failures += 1
+		print("FAIL ", label)
+
+
+func _run() -> void:
+	seed(706)
+	world = Node2D.new()
+	world.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_DISABLED
+	root.add_child(world)
+	player = Player.new()
+	var sprite := Sprite2D.new()
+	sprite.name = "Sprite"
+	player.add_child(sprite)
+	player.max_hp = 1000000.0
+	world.add_child(player)
+	player.hurt.connect(func(amount: float) -> void: damage_values.append(amount))
+	effects = Effects.new()
+	world.add_child(effects)
+	horde = Horde.new()
+	world.add_child(horde)
+	horde.setup(Rect2(0.0, 0.0, 640.0, 360.0), player, null)
+	for i in 200:
+		horde.spawn(Vector2(randf_range(90.0, 550.0), randf_range(140.0, 220.0)))
+	boss = Boss.new()
+	boss.player = player
+	boss.horde = horde
+	boss.effects = effects
+	boss.arena = horde.arena
+	world.add_child(boss)
+	boss.charge_telegraph.connect(_telegraph)
+	boss.slam_started.connect(func(_p: Vector2) -> void:
+		slams += 1
+		print("EVENT t=%.3f slam phase=%d" % [clock, boss.phase]))
+	boss.roar.connect(func(count: int) -> void:
+		roars += 1
+		print("EVENT t=%.3f roar count=%d" % [clock, count])
+		for i in count:
+			horde.spawn(boss.global_position + Vector2.from_angle(float(i) * TAU / count) * 65.0, Horde.Kind.RUNNER))
+	boss.crushed.connect(func(_p: Vector2, _dir: Vector2) -> void: crushes += 1)
+	boss.armor_plate.connect(func(_p: Vector2, _dir: Vector2) -> void: plates += 1)
+	boss.phase_changed.connect(func(phase: int) -> void: print("EVENT t=%.3f phase=%d hp=%.2f plates=%d" % [clock, phase, boss.hp, plates]))
+	boss.died.connect(func(_p: Vector2) -> void: deaths += 1)
+	boss.start(Vector2(80.0, 180.0))
+	print("SIM begin seconds=60 hz=60 shamblers=200 seed=706")
+	for frame in 3600:
+		clock = float(frame + 1) * STEP
+		if frame == 1800:
+			boss.take_damage(15999.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.global_position)
+			_check(boss.phase == 1 and is_equal_approx(boss.hp, 12000.75), "phase stays 1 above 50%")
+			boss.take_damage(1.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.global_position)
+			_check(boss.phase == 2 and is_equal_approx(boss.hp, 12000.0), "phase 2 at exactly 50%")
+			var hp_before := boss.hp
+			boss.take_damage(999.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.global_position)
+			_check(boss.hp == hp_before, "armor shedding invulnerability")
+		var segment := int(clock / 8.0) % 2
+		if segment == 0:
+			player.global_position = Vector2(320.0, 180.0) + Vector2.from_angle(clock * 0.4) * 150.0
+		else:
+			player.global_position = boss.global_position.move_toward(Vector2(320.0, 180.0), 75.0)
+		player._iframes = maxf(player._iframes - STEP, 0.0)
+		horde._physics_process(STEP)
+		_tick(STEP)
+		effects._physics_process(STEP)
+		_check(boss.global_position == boss._bounded(boss.global_position), "arena containment")
+	_check(telegraphs >= 5 and slams > 1 and roars > 0 and crushes > 0, "all attacks and crushing in 60 s")
+	_check(boss.phase == 2 and effects.particle_count() > 0, "phase 2 blood")
+	print("SIM end telegraphs=%d slams=%d roars=%d crushed=%d plates=%d" % [telegraphs, slams, roars, crushes, plates])
+	print("PERF boss_physics_avg_ms=%.6f max_ms=%.6f samples=%d" % [float(physics_usec) / samples / 1000.0, float(physics_max) / 1000.0, samples])
+	_check(float(physics_usec) / samples < 300.0, "boss average < 0.3 ms")
+	_unit_checks()
+	# Настоящая пауза дерева: прямые вызовы выше используются только для ускорения.
+	world.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_INHERIT
+	player.set_physics_process(false)
+	horde.set_physics_process(false)
+	effects.set_physics_process(false)
+	boss.start(Vector2(200.0, 180.0))
+	paused = true
+	var old_age := boss._age
+	for i in 3:
+		await physics_frame
+	_check(boss._age == old_age, "scene pause")
+	paused = false
+	print("CHECK pause PASS" if boss._age == old_age else "CHECK pause FAIL")
+	print("RESULT failures=%d" % failures)
+	quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
+
+
+func _telegraph(_from: Vector2, _to: Vector2, duration: float) -> void:
+	telegraphs += 1
+	charge_begin = clock
+	charge_wait = duration
+	print("EVENT t=%.3f telegraph phase=%d duration=%.1f" % [clock, boss.phase, duration])
+
+
+func _tick(delta: float) -> void:
+	var before := Time.get_ticks_usec()
+	boss._physics_process(delta)
+	var elapsed := Time.get_ticks_usec() - before
+	physics_usec += elapsed
+	physics_max = maxi(physics_max, elapsed)
+	samples += 1
+	if boss._state == Boss.State.CHARGE and previous_state == Boss.State.TELEGRAPH:
+		_check(clock - charge_begin + 0.00001 >= charge_wait, "full charge telegraph")
+	previous_state = boss._state
+
+
+func _reset(p := Vector2(200.0, 180.0)) -> void:
+	boss.start(p)
+	boss.horde = null
+	damage_values.clear()
+	player._iframes = 0.0
+	player.is_dead = false
+	player.global_position = boss.global_position
+
+
+func _unit_checks() -> void:
+	_reset()
+	player.position += Vector2(100.0, 0.0)
+	boss._charge_cd = 99.0
+	boss._slam_cd = 99.0
+	boss._roar_cd = 99.0
+	var old_pos := boss.position
+	boss._physics_process(0.1)
+	_check(is_equal_approx(boss.position.distance_to(old_pos), 4.0), "phase 1 speed 40")
+	boss.take_damage(16000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	boss._physics_process(1.2)
+	old_pos = boss.position
+	boss._physics_process(0.1)
+	_check(is_equal_approx(boss.position.distance_to(old_pos), 6.0), "phase 2 speed 60")
+	var old_hp := boss.hp
+	boss.take_damage(100.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	_check(is_equal_approx(old_hp - boss.hp, 100.0) and boss._flash == 0.08, "phase 2 full damage and flash")
+	_check(effects._bake.count(1) > 0, "blood bake particles")
+	print("CHECK walk_speed=40/60 phase2_damage=100% flash=0.08 blood_bake PASS")
+	_reset()
+	boss._charge_cd = 99.0
+	boss._slam_cd = 99.0
+	boss._roar_cd = 99.0
+	for i in 49:
+		player._iframes = 0.0
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values == [20.0, 20.0], "contact 20, cooldown 0.8")
+	_reset()
+	boss.take_damage(16000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	boss._physics_process(1.2)
+	boss._charge_cd = 99.0
+	boss._slam_cd = 99.0
+	boss._roar_cd = 99.0
+	boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values == [25.0], "phase 2 contact 25")
+	print("CHECK contact phase1=20 phase2=25 cooldown=0.8 PASS")
+	_reset(Vector2(100.0, 180.0))
+	player.position = Vector2(130.0, 180.0)
+	boss._charges_left = 1
+	boss._begin_telegraph()
+	for i in 60:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values.is_empty(), "charge has no windup damage")
+	boss._physics_process(0.25)
+	boss._physics_process(0.25)
+	_check(damage_values == [35.0], "swept charge 35 once")
+	for i in 5:
+		boss._physics_process(0.25)
+	_check(boss._state == Boss.State.RECOVER and boss._charge_cd == 6.0, "charge cooldown 6 after final pass")
+	var hp_at_recover := boss.hp
+	boss.take_damage(100.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	_check(is_equal_approx(hp_at_recover - boss.hp, 75.0), "recover vulnerable and armor 25%")
+	print("CHECK charge telegraph=1.0 swept_damage=35 once PASS")
+	_reset()
+	player.position += Vector2(80.0, 0.0)
+	boss._choose_attack()
+	for i in 29:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values.is_empty() and not boss._slam_wave, "slam windup no damage")
+	for i in 38:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values == [25.0], "slam ring 25 once")
+	_check(boss._slam_cd > 8.3 and boss._slam_cd < 8.5, "slam cooldown 9")
+	_reset()
+	player.position += Vector2(80.0, 0.0)
+	boss._choose_attack()
+	player._iframes = 10.0
+	for i in 67:
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(damage_values.is_empty(), "slam dash invulnerability")
+	_reset()
+	boss.take_damage(16000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	boss._physics_process(1.2)
+	player.position += Vector2(80.0, 0.0)
+	boss._choose_attack()
+	boss._physics_process(0.5)
+	_check(is_equal_approx(boss._slam_cd, 6.3), "phase 2 slam cooldown 6.3")
+	print("CHECK slam windup=0.5 radius=150 time=0.6 width=20 damage=25 once dash_safe PASS")
+	_reset()
+	boss.take_damage(16000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position)
+	boss._physics_process(1.2)
+	player.position = Vector2(500.0, 180.0)
+	var t: Array[float] = []
+	var record := func(_a: Vector2, _b: Vector2, duration: float) -> void: t.append(duration)
+	boss.charge_telegraph.connect(record)
+	boss._choose_attack()
+	var recover_frames := 0
+	var recovery_lengths: Array[int] = []
+	var last_state := boss._state
+	for i in 2000:
+		if boss._state == Boss.State.RECOVER:
+			recover_frames += 1
+		boss._physics_process(STEP)
+		if last_state == Boss.State.RECOVER and boss._state != Boss.State.RECOVER:
+			recovery_lengths.append(recover_frames)
+			recover_frames = 0
+		last_state = boss._state
+		player.position = Vector2(640.0, 360.0) - player.position
+		if recovery_lengths.size() == 3:
+			break
+	boss.charge_telegraph.disconnect(record)
+	_check(t == [0.7, 0.7, 0.7], "three fresh phase 2 telegraphs")
+	_check(recovery_lengths == [24, 24, 36], "recover 0.4/0.4/0.6")
+	print("CHECK phase2 telegraphs=%s recovery_frames=%s" % [t, recovery_lengths])
+	_reset(Vector2(622.0, 342.0))
+	player.position = Vector2(639.0, 359.0)
+	boss._charges_left = 1
+	boss._begin_telegraph()
+	boss._physics_process(1.0)
+	boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(boss._state == Boss.State.RECOVER, "wall ends charge")
+	boss.global_position = Vector2(-999.0, 999.0)
+	boss._physics_process(STEP)
+	_check(boss.position == Vector2(18.0, 342.0), "external displacement clamped")
+	_check(boss.hit_test(boss.position + Vector2(20.0, 0.0), 2.0), "hit test tangent")
+	_reset()
+	var before := deaths
+	_check(boss.take_damage(100000.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position), "lethal returns true")
+	_check(not boss.is_alive() and not boss.visible and deaths == before + 1, "death hides and emits once")
+	_check(not boss.take_damage(10.0, Vector2.RIGHT, boss.position) and deaths == before + 1, "no repeated death")
+	_check(not boss.hit_test(boss.position, 10.0), "dead hit test false")
+	print("CHECK wall_recover displacement hit_test death PASS")

+extends SceneTree
+
+var screen: Node2D
+var panels: Array[SubViewport] = []
+
+
+func _initialize() -> void:
+	call_deferred("_run")
+
+
+func _run() -> void:
+	root.size = Vector2i(1280, 720)
+	screen = Node2D.new()
+	root.add_child(screen)
+	var captions := ["PHASE 1 / ARMOR", "PHASE 2 / EXPOSED", "CHARGE / TELEGRAPH", "SLAM / WINDUP", "SLAM / WAVE", "ROAR / DISTORTION"]
+	for i in 6:
+		var viewport := SubViewport.new()
+		viewport.size = Vector2i(426, 360)
+		viewport.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_ALWAYS
+		root.add_child(viewport)
+		panels.append(viewport)
+		var back := Node2D.new()
+		viewport.add_child(back)
+		back.draw.connect(func() -> void:
+			back.draw_rect(Rect2(0, 0, 426, 360), Color(0.12, 0.135, 0.12))
+			for x in range(0, 426, 24):
+				back.draw_line(Vector2(x, 40), Vector2(x, 360), Color(0.16, 0.17, 0.15))
+			for y in range(48, 360, 24):
+				back.draw_line(Vector2(0, y), Vector2(426, y), Color(0.16, 0.17, 0.15))
+			back.draw_rect(Rect2(0, 0, 426, 40), Color(0.045, 0.055, 0.05))
+			back.draw_string(ThemeDB.fallback_font, Vector2(16, 27), captions[i], HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 18, Color(0.85, 0.86, 0.77)))
+		var boss := Boss.new()
+		boss.arena = Rect2(0, 40, 426, 320)
+		viewport.add_child(boss)
+		boss.start(Vector2(213, 200))
+		boss.set_physics_process(false)
+		if i <= 1:
+			boss.scale = Vector2.ONE * 3.0
+		if i == 1:
+			boss.phase = 2
+		if i == 2:
+			boss._state = Boss.State.TELEGRAPH
+			boss._age = 0.78
+			boss._charge_from = boss.position
+			boss._charge_target = Vector2(408, 120)
+			boss._facing = (boss._charge_target - boss.position).normalized()
+		if i == 3 or i == 4:
+			boss._state = Boss.State.SLAM
+			boss._slam_pos = boss.position
+			boss._age = 0.35 if i == 3 else 0.96
+			boss._slam_wave = i == 4
+		if i == 5:
+			boss.phase = 2
+			boss._state = Boss.State.ROAR
+			boss._age = 0.5
+		boss.queue_redraw()
+		var view := Sprite2D.new()
+		view.centered = false
+		view.texture = viewport.get_texture()
+		view.position = Vector2((i % 3) * 427, (i / 3) * 360)
+		screen.add_child(view)
+	for frame in 8:
+		await process_frame
+	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
+	var image := root.get_texture().get_image()
+	var error := image.save_png(OS.get_cmdline_user_args()[0])
+	print("PREVIEW save_png=", error, " size=", image.get_size())
+	quit(error)
